======================================================================
PHYSICS KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Physics
Definition: The branch of science that studies matter, energy, motion and forces.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Physics is an important topic in physics.
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 2
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Displacement
Definition: The shortest distance between initial and final position.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Displacement is an important topic in physics.
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 3
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Velocity
Definition: Rate of change of displacement.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
A car travels at 20 m/s.
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 4
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Acceleration
Definition: Rate of change of velocity.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Free-fall acceleration on Earth is approximately 9.8 m/s².
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 5
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Force
Definition: A push or pull acting on an object.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Applying 10 N to push a box.
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 6
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Newton's First Law
Definition: An object remains at rest or in uniform motion unless acted upon by a net external force.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Newton's First Law is an important topic in physics.
Related Concepts:
Mechanics, Thermodynamics, Electricity, Magnetism, Waves, Optics

Entry 7
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Newton's Second Law
Definition: Force equals mass multiplied by acceleration (F = ma).
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Newton's Second Law is an important topic in physics.

Entry 10
Category: Mechanics
Concept: Work
Definition: Product of force and displacement in the direction of the force.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:

- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.

Category: Waves, Optics & Modern Physics
Concept: Reflection
Definition: Bouncing of light from a surface.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in physics.
- Widely used in science, engineering and technology.
- Frequently asked in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
A mirror reflects incident light.
